11px\">Fungal infections</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Scabies</li></ul><a name=\"opt-item.head-eye-ent-findings\"> </a><p><b>Answer options for head-eye-ent-findings </b></p><ul><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">No significant findings</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Icterus</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Thrush</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Oral Kaposi sarcoma</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Gingivitis</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Otitis media</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Ear discharge</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Oral ulcer</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Abnormal fundoscopy</li></ul><a name=\"opt-item.clinical-assessment\"> </a><p><b>Answer options for clinical-assessment </b></p><ul><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Asymptomatic</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Symptomatic</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">AIDS-defining illness</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Opportunistic infection</li></ul><a style=\"font-size: 11px\">WHO stage 3</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">WHO stage 4</li></ul><a name=\"opt-item.enrol-in-service\"> </a><p><b>Answer options for enrol-in-service </b></p><ul><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">General medical follow-up</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">ARV therapy</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Advanced HIV disease management</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Pending laboratory results</li></ul><a name=\"opt-item.art-plan\"> </a><p><b>Answer options for art-plan </b></p><ul><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Ongoing monitoring; ART postponed for clinical reasons</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Change treatment</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Restart treatment</li><li style=\"font-size: 11px\">Start new treatment</li></ul><a
